Let's plan run() concretely.

We can implement several local optimizers:
A. Smooth projected gradient (L-BFGS maybe) with entropic max and exact projection. Use high n (e.g., 400 from parent, maybe 600/800).
B. Active-set subgradient with cubic? Use p from top lags.
C. Binary/ternary local search using exact delta for pair swaps (integer/binary) to find near-binary initial/bases.
D. Multi-resolution interpolation and downsampling.
E. Random restarts/noise with local.

1. Start with initial_h_values (n=400). Make multiple candidates:

2. For each candidate, run "polish":
   a. Projected gradient on logsumexp with α schedule (100, 1000, 1e4, 1e5, 1e6, maybe 1e7) using line search/backtracking.
   b. Pair-move line search sweeps (top-gradient pairs) for exact improvement.
   c. Repeat gradient with high α/pair moves.
3. Track best. Return best with n.

Feasibility/projection:
def make_feasible(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    # water filling via bisection with 50 iterations
    # Use sum of clipped y - tau.
    lo = h.min() - 1.0
    hi = h.max() + 1.0
    # We want sum(clip(h - tau,0,1)) = target. Since increasing tau decreases sum.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       s=np.clip(h-mid,0,1).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid else hi=mid
    tau=(lo+hi)/2
    out=np.clip(h-tau,0,1)
    # adjust remaining tiny residual by scaling? But bisection may leave 1e-14.
    # Use simple correction on values with room:
    diff=target-out.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       # distribute to non-bound values
       mask=(out>1e-12)&(out<1-1e-12)
       if mask.sum()>0:
           out[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
           out=np.clip(out,0,1)
    # final exact-ish: set one non-bound to absorb residual?
    out=np.clip(np.nan_to_num(out,0,1),0,1)
    return out

But bisection sum uses h - tau, with tau positive? If target lower than sum at lo? Let's test: lo=min-1, h-lo≥1 for min? For all h >= lo+1 => h-lo>=1 -> clip=1 sum=n. hi=max+1 => h-hi≤0 sum=0. Good. We want target between 0,n. If sum(mid)>target, need larger tau => lo=mid? Wait lo gives high sum, hi gives low sum. If sum > target, root to right (larger tau), so lo=mid. If sum < target, root left, hi=mid. Good.

At end out sum maybe target within bisection tolerance. We can compute exact residual and add to a coordinate with most fractional room. But many values at bounds. Choose idx = argmin(|out-0.5|) maybe. residual tiny. If out[idx]+res within [0,1], set.

But for performance in gradient descent, calling 60 bisection each iter costly. We can use faster approximate projection:
def project_fast(y,n):
   h=np.clip(y,0,1)
   target=n/2
   # if sum close, use linear scaling with clipping loop max 10
   for _ in range(20):
      s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
      if abs(diff) <1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
          room=1-h
          sr=room.sum()
          if sr < 1e-15: break
          h += diff/sr*room
      else:
          sr=s
          if sr < 1e-15: break
          h += diff/sr*h
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   return h
This is previous. It may converge fast for near feasible. Use in iterations; final exact. Could be okay. However clipping after scaling may create error; loop 20. For n=400, 1000 iter ->8M ops, fine. Use 30 iterations.

But gradient line search uses projection each eval; fast scaling may be enough. At final, exact projection.

def c5_from_h(h):

   return float(np.max(c)*dx), c

Build G efficiently:
def build_gradient_matrix(h,n):
   v=1-h
   L=2*n-1
   G=np.zeros((L,n), dtype=float)
   vrev=v[::-1]
   for i in range(n):
       G[i:i+n, i] += vrev
       G[n-1-i:2*n-1-i, i] -= h
   return G
Check slice end for B: 2*n-1-i (exclusive). For i=0 end=2n-1? L=2n-1, valid exclusive L. i=0: [n-1:L] length n. i=n-1: [0:n] length n. Good. Values forward h? B derivation: for i=4,n=5, slice [0:5] subtract h[0..4] for k=-4..0 giving h[0..4]? k=-4: h[0]; correct. For i=0: slice [4:9] subtract h[0..4] for k=0..4: h[0..4]. Good. A: G[i:i+n] += vrev: for i=0, t0..4 k=-4..0, v[0-k]=v4..v0 = vrev; for i=4,t4..8 k=0..4, v4..v0. Good. So G correct.

Implement `project_fast` efficiently:
def project_fast(h, n):
    h = np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0.0,1.0)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(30):
        s = h.sum()
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            sr = room.sum()
            if sr < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff/sr)*room
        else:
            sr = s
            if sr < 1e-15:
                break
            h = h + (diff/sr)*h
        h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h
This loop uses new arrays each iter. For gradient many calls, okay. But for LP line search 12 evals * many LP, maybe okay. Could optimize with in-place? Not necessary.

Project exact with bisection:
def project_exact(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(h,float),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    # Use bisection 70
    lo=h.min()-1.0; hi=h.max()+1.0
    for _ in range(70):
       mid=(lo+hi)*0.5
       s=np.clip(h-mid,0.0,1.0).sum()
       if s > target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    x=np.clip(h-(lo+hi)*0.5,0.0,1.0)
    # Adjust residual
    diff=target-x.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-13:
        # find interior
        mask=(x>1e-12)&(x<1-1e-12)
        if mask.any():
            x= x.copy()
            x[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
            x=np.clip(x,0,1)
        else:
            # distribute to all? should not
            x += diff/n
            x=np.clip(x,0,1)
    return x
Bisection with 70 vector ops per final only. Good.

Smooth objective:
def smooth_obj_grad(h, c, alpha):
    n=len(h); dx=2.0/n; L=2*n-1
    m = c.max()
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    p = np.exp(z)
    psum = p.sum()
    if psum <=0 or not np.isfinite(psum):
       # fallback uniform over top active
       idx=np.where(z >= -1e-16)[0]
       p=np.zeros(L); p[idx]=1.0; psum=idx.size
       if psum==0: p[n-1]=1; psum=1
    p /= psum
    F = m + (np.log(psum)/alpha) # counts; actual C = dx*F? Since z=alpha*(c-m), LSE = m + logsumexp/alpha. Objective counts. If we want dx, later.
    v=1-h
    vrev? For convolution needs p direct.
    A = np.convolve(v, p, mode='full')
    B = np.convolve(h, p[::-1], mode='full')
    grad = dx * (A[n-1:2*n-1] - B[n-1:2*n-1])
    return F, grad, p
But if alpha very large, log(psum)/alpha may overflow? z max 0, psum <= L, log finite. If c-m large negative *alpha underflows to -inf, exp 0; psum maybe 1. Good.
But if alpha*(c-m) *? c-m can be e.g. -10, alpha=1e7 => z=-1e8 underflow -inf, exp 0. okay.
Should we use C=c*dx in α? If α in counts, F count. Gradient units dx. Fine.

Gradient polish:
def gradient_polish(h, c, alpha_sched, deadline, base_step=0.5, max_round=2000):
   n=len(h); dx=2/n; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_count=c.max()
   for alpha in alpha_sched:
      # maybe reduce step with alpha? step_scale = min(1, 100/alpha?) not.
      h_curr=h.copy(); c_curr=c.copy(); count_curr=best? Use current.
      # Use previous step maybe 0.5
      step=0.5
      for it in range(max_round):
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         F, grad, p = smooth_obj_grad(h_curr,c_curr,alpha)
         norm2 = float(grad.dot(grad))
         if norm2 < 1e-22: break
         # maybe normalize direction? SLSQP used raw grad. Step should be small.
         # Use max_step based on gradient: since grad ~dx, step 1 okay. But if high alpha grad may only active, magnitude ~dx*active_count? Could be 0.005*? Actually p one active, A-B sums one lag over n values: up to n*0.005=1; step1 maybe large. Use step = min(step, 0.1*(max(1-h).sum()/n)? no)
         accepted=False
         for _ in range(20):
            h_new = project_fast(h_curr - step*grad, n)
            c_new = eval_corr(h_new)
            count_new = c_new.max()
            F_new,_,_ = smooth_obj_grad(h_new,c_new,alpha)
            # Armijo on F counts
            if F_new <= F + 1e-4*step*(-norm2) - 1e-15:
                accepted=True; break
            step *=0.5
         if not accepted:
            step *=0.5
            if step < 1e-12: break
            # accept best found? maybe break
            break
         h_curr=h_new; c_curr=c_new; step *= 1.1? increase? keep
         if count_new < best_count - 1e-14:
             best_count=count_new; best_h=h_new.copy(); best_c=c_new.copy()
      h=batch best? continue
   return best_h,best_c,best_count

Pair polish function:
def pair_polish(h,c,deadline,max_sweeps=200,pair_alpha=50.0):
   n, L, dx...
   best_h, best_c, best_count.
   for sweep in range(max_sweeps):
      if time done break
      # active weights for gradient (not p? use top alpha)
      cmax=c.max()
      z=pair_alpha*(c-cmax)
      p=np.exp(z); psum=p.sum(); if psum==0 active top; p/=psum
      v=1-h
      A=np.convolve(v,p,'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
      B=np.convolve(h,p[::-1],'full')[n-1:2*n-1]
      grad=dx*(A-B)
      order=np.argsort(grad)
      lows=order[:8]; highs=order[-8:][::-1]
      pairs=set? We can generate list up to 100: all combinations? 8*8=64.
      tried=set()
      improved_sweep=False
      for i in lows:
        for j in highs:
           if i==j: continue
           key=(int(min(i,j)),int(max(i,j))) if? Pair ordered matters? We line search both directions; order can be canonical to avoid duplicate.
           if key in tried: continue
           added
           # build q and e
           q=np.zeros(L)
           vrev=v[::-1]
           q[i:i+n] += vrev
           q[n-1-i:2*n-1-i] -= h
           q[j:j+n] -= vrev
           q[n-1-j:2*n-1-j] += h
           t1=n-1+i-j; t2=n-1+j-i
           # top filter
           top = c >= cmax - 1e-10
           q_top = q[top]
           can_pos = q_top.max() <= 1e-12
           can_neg = q_top.min() >= -1e-12
           if not can_pos and not can_neg:
              continue
           # bounds
           pos = min(1-h[i], h[j])
           neg = min(h[i], 1-h[j])
           if can_pos and can_neg: lo=-neg; hi=pos
           elif can_pos: lo=0.0; hi=pos
           else: lo=-neg; hi=0.0
           if hi <= lo: continue
           # If possible first-order improvement: for δ sign, min q_top? Already.
           # Golden search
           best_delta, best_count = minimize_pair_delta(c,q,t1,t2,lo,hi)
           if best_count < current_count - 1e-12:
              h[i]+=best_delta; h[j]-=best_delta; c=eval_corr(h) (or c + update); current_count=best_count; improved_sweep=True; update best
              # break and recompute grad? For efficiency, break pairs loop? If accept, grad/c changed; we can break to new sweep.
              break
        if improved_sweep: break
      if not improved_sweep:
         # maybe try random fractional pairs? Or break after few stagnant sweeps
         stagnant+=1; if stagnant>3 break
         else continue
   return best

Minimize_pair_delta function:
def pair_delta_eval(c,q,t1,t2,delta):
   tmp = c + delta*q
   dd=delta*delta
   if t1==t2: tmp=t1+=dd else tmp[t1]+=dd; tmp[t2]+=dd
   return float(tmp.max())
But allocating tmp each eval. Could preallocate in function? Function can't hold across calls? We'll allocate.
Golden:
def minimize_pair_delta(c,q,t1,t2,lo,hi):
   if hi-lo < 1e-14: d=0.5*(lo+hi); return d, pair_eval...
   gr=(math.sqrt(5)-1)/2  # 0.618? Standard: c=hi - gr*(hi-lo); d=lo+gr*(hi-lo). After iterations c,d.
   c1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); c2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
   f1=eval(c1); f2=eval(c2)
   for _ in range(25):
      if f1 < f2: hi=c2; c2=c1; f2=f1; c1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f1=eval(c1)
      else: lo=c1; c1=c2; f1=f2; c2=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f2=eval(c2)
   mid=0.5*(lo+hi); fmid=eval(mid)
   # also evaluate endpoints maybe
   for d in [lo,hi,mid,c1,c2]: if eval<d keep.
   return best

Let's implement `lp_polish` with high precision and multiple rho:
- rho list [0.1,0.05,0.02,0.01,0.005,0.002]. For each rho, iterate until stall. This gradually refines.
- At each rho, max qps e.g. 100 or until no improvement for 5. After stall, reduce rho.
- Include random perturb: if no improvement for many, add noise scaled rho*0.5 to h projected, then continue (but keep best).

Pseudo:
```
def lp_polish(h0,c0,n,deadline,max_total=800):
  h=project_exact(h0,n); c=eval_corr(h); count=c.max()
  best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_count=count
  rng=np.random.RandomState(123)
  rho=0.1
  stall=0
  while time.time()<deadline and rho>=0.001:
     G=build_G(h,n)
     ... linprog
     if not success: stall+=1; rho*=0.7; continue
     delta=res.x[:n]; zpred=res.x[n]
     # line search fixed gammas
     best_gamma,best_count_new,best_h_new,best_c_new = line_search_dir(...)
     if best_count_new < count - 1e-12:
         h,c,count=best...
         if count<best_count: update
         stall=0
         # maybe if improvement very small and rho>0.01, reduce rho
         if (count - best_count) < 1e-8? no
     else:
         stall+=1
         if stall==1:
             # try random perturbation of current h or best
             if rng.random()<0.5: h=project_exact(h + 0.01*rho*rng.randn(n),n); c=eval...
         else:
             rho *= 0.6; stall=0
  return best
```

```
def slsqp_polish(h0,c0,n,deadline):
    n=len(h0); best ...
    alphas=[2000,20000,200000,2000000]
    bounds=[(0,1)]*n
    cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':_eq_func,'args':(n,)}]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time>deadline: break
       try:
          res=minimize(_obj, h, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons, args=(alpha,n), options={'maxiter':300,'ftol':1e-15})
          h=project_exact(res.x,n)
          c=eval_corr(h); count=c.max()
          if count<best: update
          # If SLSQP didn't improve, still use res? maybe use best as next start.
          h=best_h.copy()
       except: pass
    return best
```

```
def line_search_direction(h, c, d, cur_count, max_evals=10):
    n=len(h); dx=2/n
    # check d norm
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)) or np.linalg.norm(d) < 1e-14: return None...
    gammas = np.array([1.0,0.8,0.6,0.5,0.4,0.3,0.2,0.15,0.1,0.07,0.05,0.03,0.02,0.01])
    if d's max maybe small, but okay.
    best_g=0; best_h=None; best_c=None; best_count=cur_count
    for g in gammas:
       hn=h+g*d
       # if any out of box due numerical, break to smaller? Since d feasible at1 should not; if fail reduce
       mn=hn.min(); mx=hn.max()
       if mn < -1e-12 or mx > 1+1e-12:
           # scale g? just continue smaller
           if mn<0 or mx>1:
               # compute max feasible g by ratios
               continue
       hn=project_fast(hn,n)
       cn=eval_corr(hn); cnt=cn.max()
       if cnt < best_count - 1e-14: update
       if g < 0.01: break
    if best_g>0:
       # optional refine: use golden on [0, best_g*1.2] maybe
       return best_h,best_c,best_count
    return None
```

Let's implement `lp_polish` with QP count limit and time:
```
def lp_polish(h0, c0, n, deadline, max_qps=1000, seed=1, rho_start=0.1, rho_min=1e-4):
   h=project_exact(h0,n); c=eval_corr(h); count=c.max()
   best_h, best_c, best_count = ...
   rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
   rho = rho_start
   stall = 0
   qps = 0
   while time.time() < deadline and qps < max_qps and rho >= rho_min:
       G = build_G(h,n)
       L = 2*n-1
       A_ub = np.empty((L,n+1)); A_ub[:,:n]=G; A_ub[:,n]=-1.0
       b_ub = -c
       A_eq = np.zeros((1,n+1)); A_eq[0,:n]=1.0
       c_obj = np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
       bounds=[]
       for i in range(n):
           lo = max(-h[i], -rho)
           hi = min(1.0-h[i], rho)
           if hi < lo: hi=lo
           bounds.append((lo,hi))
       bounds.append((0.0, None))
       try:
           res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.array([0.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs')
       except Exception:
           res=None
       qps +=1
       if res is not None and res.status ==0:
           d=res.x[:n]
           # clean tiny
           d[np.abs(d)<1e-12]=0.0
           zpred=float(res.x[-1])
           if zpred < count - 1e-12:
               out = line_search_direction(h,c,d,count)
               if out is not None and out[2] < count - 1e-13:
                   h,c,count=out...
                   if count<best_count: update
                   stall=0
                   # if improvement small, maybe reduce rho
                   if count - zpred? If (actual - best_count) < 1e-6 and rho>0.02: rho*=0.7
                   continue
           # if no improvement, maybe no gap
       stall +=1
       if stall >= 3:
           # perturb
           noise_scale = max(1e-5, 0.1*rho)
           # perturb current or best? propose current + noise from best
           if rng.random() < 0.5:
              h_try = project_exact(h + noise_scale*rng.randn(n), n)
           else:
              h_try = project_exact(best_h + noise_scale*rng.randn(n), n)
           c_try=eval_corr(h_try); cnt_try=c_try.max()
           # accept perturb if not too bad (e.g., count <= best_count+0.01)
           if cnt_try <= best_count + 0.02:
               h,c,count=h_try,c_try,cnt_try
           else:
               h,c,count=best_h.copy(),best_c.copy(),best_count
           rho = max(rho_min, rho*0.7)
           stall=0
       elif stall ==1:
           # reduce rho a bit
           rho = max(rho_min, rho*0.8)
   return best_h,best_c,best_count
```

```
def max_feasible_gamma(h,d):
    pos=d>1e-15; neg=d<-1e-15
    if pos.any(): u1=np.min((1-h[pos])/d[pos])
    else u1=1.0
    if neg.any(): u2=np.min(h[neg]/(-d[neg]))
    else u2=1.0
    return max(0,min(1,u1,u2))
```

```
def gradient_polish(h0,c0,n,deadline,max_iters_per_alpha=400):
    h=project_exact(h0,n); c=eval_corr(h); count=...
    best...
    alphas=[100,500,2000,10000,50000,200000,1000000]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time>deadline break
       step=1.0
       for it in range(max_iters_per_alpha):
           if time>deadline: break
           F,grad,p=smooth_max(h,c,alpha)
           norm2=float(grad.dot(grad))
           if norm2 < 1e-24: break
           accepted=False
           trial_step=step
           for _ in range(12):
              h_new=project_fast(h - trial_step*grad, n)
              c_new=eval_corr(h_new); cnt_new=c_new.max()
              if cnt_new < count - 1e-15:
                 accepted=True; break
              F_new,_,_=smooth_max(h_new,c_new,alpha)
              if F_new < F - 1e-10:
                 accepted=True; break
              trial_step *= 0.5
           if not accepted:
              step = trial_step * 0.5
              if step < 1e-12: break
              continue
           h,c,count = h_new,c_new,cnt_new
           step = min(step*1.1, 2.0)
           if count < best_count - 1e-14: update
       h=best_h? maybe continue from best to not drift? If current not best, maybe use best.
       # after angle, set h=best_h to continue from best
       h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); count=best_count
    return best
```

Now, `resample_h`:
```
def resample_h(h,n,m):
   if m == n: return h.copy()
   xo=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
   xn=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,m)
   out=np.interp(xn,xo,h)
   return project_exact(out,m)
```

Potential bug: In `project_fast`, using `h = h + (diff / sr) * room` may break exact sum after clip. Loop enough. For n large, 30 iterations. For LP line search many calls, may be slow. We can make `project_fast` faster by using in-place:
```
def project_fast(h,n):
    h=np.clip(np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True),0,1)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(20):
       s=h.sum()
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h
          sr=room.sum()
          if sr<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/sr)*room
       else:
          sr=s
          if sr<1e-15: break
          h += (diff/sr)*h
       np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
```
In-place. Good.

`project_exact` bisection uses `np.clip(h - mid,0,1)` allocations 70; okay final. Could use project_fast then bisection 30? Fine.

```
def evaluate_direction(h,d,gamma):
    if gamma <=0: return None
    hn = h + gamma*d
    if hn.min() < -1e-10 or hn.max() > 1+1e-10:
        # try clip if small
        if hn.min() < -1e-8 or hn.max() > 1+1e-8:
            return None
    hn = np.clip(hn,0,1)
    n=h.size
    # quick sum adjust
    if abs(hn.sum()-0.5*n)>1e-10:
        hn=project_fast(hn,n)
    cn=eval_corr(hn)
    return hn,cn,float(cn.max())
```

`smooth_max`:
```
def smooth_max(h, c, alpha):
    n = h.size
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    p = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        idx = np.where(z >= -1e-9)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            p[int(c.argmax())] = 1.0
        else:
            p[idx] = 1.0
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 0.0:
            p[0]=1.0; ps=1.0
    p = p / ps
    F = m + np.log(ps) / alpha
    v = 1.0 - h
    A = np.convolve(v, p, mode='full')
    B = np.convolve(h, p[::-1], mode='full')
    grad = A[n-1:2*n-1] - B[n-1:2*n-1]
    return F, grad, p
```
If n=0, not. For n=1, c length1, convolve works slice? n-1=0, 2n-1=1. okay. But problem n large.

`_slsqp_obj`:
```
def _slsqp_obj(x, alpha, n):
    h=np.clip(np.asarray(x,dtype=float),0,1)
    c=eval_corr(h)
    F,_,_=smooth_max(h,c,alpha)
    return float(F)
```
`_slsqp_jac`: return grad (float64).

`_slsqp_con`: `return float(np.asarray(x).sum() - 0.5*n)`.

`slsqp_polish`:
```
def slsqp_polish(h0,c0,n,deadline,maxiter=150):
    h=np.array(project_exact(h0,n),float)
    c=eval_corr(h); count=float(c.max())
    best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c.copy(); best_count=count
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons=({'type':'eq','fun':_slsqp_con,'args':(n,)},)
    alphas=[2000,20000,200000,1000000]
    for alpha in alphas:
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       h_start=best_h.copy()
       try:
          res=minimize(_slsqp_obj,h_start,args=(alpha,n),jac=_slsqp_jac,method='SLSQP',bounds=bounds,constraints=cons,options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
       except Exception:
          continue
       hx=res.x if hasattr(res,'x') else None
       if hx is not None:
          hx=project_exact(hx,n)
          cx=eval_corr(hx); cntx=float(cx.max())
          if cntx < best_count - 1e-14:
              best_h=hx.copy(); best_c=cx.copy(); best_count=cntx
          # maybe if res.status==2 success? Accept best only; continue from best
       # Also if current h after SLSQP (even if count worsens) but smooth F? We only track exact.
    return best_h,best_c,best_count
```

```
   alphas=[100,1000,10000,100000,500000]
   step=1.0
   for alpha in alphas:
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      for it in range(400):
         if time.time()>deadline: break
         F,grad,p=smooth_max(h,c,alpha)
         norm2=float(np.dot(grad,grad))
         if norm2<1e-24: break
         trial=step
         accepted=False
         best_trial=trial
         best_hn=None; best_cn=None; best_cnt=count
         for _ in range(10):
             hn=project_fast(h - trial*grad,n)
             cn=eval_corr(hn); cnt=cn.max()
             if cnt < best_cnt - 1e-14:
                 best_cnt=cnt; best_hn=hn; best_cn=cn; best_trial=trial
             Fnew,_,_=smooth_max(hn,cn,alpha)
             if (cnt < count - 1e-14) or (Fnew < F - 1e-10 and cnt < count + 1e-9):
                 accepted=True; break
             trial*=0.5
         if not accepted:
             step=trial*0.5
             if step<1e-12: break
             # maybe still use best_trial if improves? if best_hn: accept? It improved count, but condition cnt<count true would accept; so no.
             continue
         h,c,count=best_hn,best_cn,best_cnt? If accepted due F but count not improve, assigned best_hn with count maybe. Use accepted variables.
         step=min(step*1.05,2.0)
         if count<best_count -1e-14: update
      # after alpha, reset to best? If current count worse, use best.
      if count > best_count + 1e-8:
          h=best_h.copy(); c=best_c.copy(); count=best_count
   return best
```

```
    v = 1.0 - h

    for i in range(n):

        G[n-1-i:2*n-1-i, i] -= h
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog

np.seterr(over='ignore', under='ignore', invalid='ignore', divide='ignore')


def eval_corr(h):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')


def c5_of_corr(c, n):
    return float(np.max(c) * (2.0 / n))


def project_fast(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(24):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            sr = float(room.sum())
            if sr < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / sr) * room
        else:
            sr = s
            if sr < 1e-15:
                break
            h += (diff / sr) * h
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def project_exact(h, n):
    h = np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True)
    h = np.nan_to_num(h, nan=0.5, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * n

    lo = float(h.min()) - 1.0
    hi = float(h.max()) + 1.0
    for _ in range(70):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        s = float(np.clip(h - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if s > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    x = np.clip(h - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)

    diff = target - float(x.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-13:
        mask = (x > 1e-12) & (x < 1.0 - 1e-12)
        if np.any(mask):
            x = x.copy()
            x[mask] += diff / float(np.sum(mask))
            np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
        else:
            x += diff / max(1, n)
            np.clip(x, 0.0, 1.0, out=x)
    return x


def smooth_max(h, c, alpha):
    n = int(h.size)
    if n <= 0:
        return 0.0, -h, np.zeros_like(c)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    z = alpha * (c - m)
    p = np.exp(z)
    ps = float(p.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 0.0:
        p = np.zeros_like(c)
        idx = np.where(z >= -1e-8)[0]
        if idx.size == 0:
            p[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        else:
            p[idx] = 1.0
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 0.0:
            p[0] = 1.0
            ps = 1.0
    p = p / ps

    F = m + np.log(ps) / alpha
    v = 1.0 - h
    A = np.convolve(v, p, mode='full')
    B = np.convolve(h, p[::-1], mode='full')
    grad = A[n - 1:2 * n - 1] - B[n - 1:2 * n - 1]
    return F, grad, p


def _slsqp_obj(x, alpha, n):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    F, _, _ = smooth_max(h, c, alpha)
    return float(F)


def _slsqp_jac(x, alpha, n):
    h = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    _, grad, _ = smooth_max(h, c, alpha)
    return grad


def _slsqp_con(x, n):
    return float(np.asarray(x, dtype=float).sum() - 0.5 * n)


def slsqp_polish(h0, c0, n, deadline):
    h = project_exact(h0, n)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    count = float(c.max())

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_count = count

    rem = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
    if rem < 25.0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_count

    if rem < 140.0:
        alphas = [50000, 500000]
        maxiter = 60
    elif rem < 360.0:
        alphas = [2000, 20000, 200000]
        maxiter = 110
    else:
        alphas = [2000, 20000, 200000, 1000000]
        maxiter = 170

    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = ({'type': 'eq', 'fun': _slsqp_con, 'args': (n,)},)

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 5.0:
            break
        h_start = best_h.copy()
        try:
            res = minimize(
                _slsqp_obj,
                h_start,
                args=(alpha, n),
                jac=_slsqp_jac,
                method='SLSQP',
                bounds=bounds,
                constraints=cons,
                options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        if hasattr(res, 'x') and res.x is not None:
            hx = project_exact(res.x, n)
            cx = eval_corr(hx)
            cntx = float(cx.max())
            if cntx < best_count - 1e-14:
                best_h = hx.copy()
                best_c = cx.copy()
                best_count = cntx

    return best_h, best_c, best_count


def gradient_polish(h0, c0, n, deadline):
    h = project_exact(h0, n)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    count = float(c.max())

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_count = count

    if time.time() > deadline - 20.0:
        return best_h, best_c, best_count

    alphas = [100, 1000, 10000, 100000, 500000]
    step = 1.0

    for alpha in alphas:
        if time.time() > deadline - 10.0:
            break
        for _ in range(500):
            if time.time() > deadline - 10.0:
                break
            F, grad, _ = smooth_max(h, c, alpha)
            norm2 = float(np.dot(grad, grad))
            if norm2 < 1e-24:
                break

            trial = step
            accepted = False
            cand_h = h
            cand_c = c
            cand_count = count

            for __ in range(12):
                hn = project_fast(h - trial * grad, n)
                cn = eval_corr(hn)
                cntn = float(cn.max())

                Fnew, _, _ = smooth_max(hn, cn, alpha)
                if (cntn < count - 1e-15) or (Fnew < F - 1e-11 and cntn < count + 1e-10):
                    accepted = True
                    cand_h, cand_c, cand_count = hn, cn, cntn
                    break
                trial *= 0.5

            if not accepted:
                step = 0.5 * trial
                if step < 1e-13:
                    break
                continue

            h, c, count = cand_h, cand_c, cand_count
            step = min(step * 1.05, 2.0)

            if count < best_count - 1e-14:
                best_h = h.copy()
                best_c = c.copy()
                best_count = count

        if count > best_count + 1e-8:
            h = best_h.copy()
            c = best_c.copy()
            count = best_count

    return best_h, best_c, best_count


def build_G(h, n):
    L = 2 * n - 1
    G = np.zeros((L, n), dtype=float)
    v = 1.0 - h
    vrev = v[::-1]
    for i in range(n):
        G[i:i + n, i] += vrev
        G[n - 1 - i:2 * n - 1 - i, i] -= h
    return G


def max_feasible_gamma(h, d):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    ub = 1.0
    pos = d > 1e-15
    if np.any(pos):
        r = (1.0 - h[pos]) / d[pos]
        ub = min(ub, float(np.min(r)))
    neg = d < -1e-15
    if np.any(neg):
        r = h[neg] / (-d[neg])
        ub = min(ub, float(np.min(r)))
    if not np.isfinite(ub) or ub < 0.0:
        return 0.0
    return min(1.0, ub)


def evaluate_direction(h, d, gamma):
    if gamma <= 0.0:
        return None
    n = int(h.size)
    hn = h + gamma * d
    if hn.min() < -1e-9 or hn.max() > 1.0 + 1e-9:
        return None
    np.clip(hn, 0.0, 1.0, out=hn)
    if abs(float(hn.sum()) - 0.5 * n) > 1e-10:
        hn = project_fast(hn, n)
    cn = eval_corr(hn)
    return hn, cn, float(cn.max())


def line_search_direction(h, d, cur_count):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    n = int(h.size)
    if n == 0 or d.size != n:
        return None
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
        return None
    if np.dot(d, d) < 1e-26:
        return None

    maxg = max_feasible_gamma(h, d)
    if maxg < 1e-10:
        return None

    base = np.array([1.0, 0.8, 0.6, 0.5, 0.4, 0.3, 0.22, 0.16,
                     0.11, 0.08, 0.055, 0.035, 0.022, 0.015, 0.008, 0.004, 0.002])
    gammas = maxg * base
    gammas = gammas[gammas > 0.0]

    best_g = 0.0
    best_h = None
    best_c = None
    best_count = cur_count

    for g in gammas:
        out = evaluate_direction(h, d, g)
        if out is None:
            continue
        hn, cn, cnt = out
        if cnt < best_count - 1e-15:
            best_count = cnt
            best_g = g
            best_h = hn
            best_c = cn

    if best_g > 0.0:
        for frac in (0.5, 0.7, 0.85, 1.15, 1.35, 1.6, 2.0):
            g = best_g * frac
            if g <= 0.0 or g > maxg:
                continue
            if abs(g - best_g) < maxg * 1e-3:
                continue
            out = evaluate_direction(h, d, g)
            if out is None:
                continue
            hn, cn, cnt = out
            if cnt < best_count - 1e-15:
                best_count = cnt
                best_g = g
                best_h = hn
                best_c = cn

    if best_h is None:
        return None
    return best_h, best_c, best_count


def solve_lp(obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, time_limit):
    try:
        opts = {'time_limit': float(max(1.0, time_limit)), 'presolve': True}
        return linprog(
            obj,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=b_eq,
            bounds=bounds,
            method='highs',
            options=opts,
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            return linprog(
                obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method='highs',
            )
        except Exception:
            try:
                return linprog(
                    obj,
                    A_ub=A_ub,
                    b_ub=b_ub,
                    A_eq=A_eq,
                    b_eq=b_eq,
                    bounds=bounds,
                )
            except Exception:
                return None


def lp_polish(h0, c0, n, deadline, max_qps=1000, seed=1, rho_start=0.1, qp_time_limit=20.0):
    h = project_exact(h0, n)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    count = float(c.max())

    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_count = count

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    rho = float(rho_start)
    rho_min = 1e-5
    stall = 0
    qps = 0
    fails = 0
    L = 2 * n - 1

    while time.time() < deadline - 3.0 and qps < max_qps and rho >= rho_min:
        count = float(c.max())
        if count < best_count - 1e-14:
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c.copy()
            best_count = count

        G = build_G(h, n)
        A_ub = np.empty((L, n + 1), dtype=float)
        A_ub[:, :n] = G
        A_ub[:, n] = -1.0
        b_ub = -np.asarray(c, dtype=float)

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=float)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([0.0])

        obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
        obj[n] = 1.0

        bounds = []
        for val in h:
            lo = max(-float(val), -rho)
            hi = min(1.0 - float(val), rho)
            if hi < lo:
                hi = lo
            bounds.append((float(lo), float(hi)))
        bounds.append((0.0, float(count + 1.0)))

        tl = min(float(qp_time_limit), max(2.0, deadline - time.time() - 4.0))
        res = solve_lp(obj, A_ub, b_ub, A_eq, b_eq, bounds, tl)
        qps += 1

        if res is None:
            fails += 1
            if fails >= 6:
                break
            rho = max(rho_min, rho * 0.75)
            continue

        fails = 0
        improved = False

        if getattr(res, 'status', None) == 0 and hasattr(res, 'x') and res.x is not None:
            d = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float).copy()
            if np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                d[np.abs(d) < 1e-13] = 0.0
                zpred = float(res.x[n])
                if np.isfinite(zpred) and zpred < count - 1e-10:
                    out = line_search_direction(h, d, count)
                    if out is not None and out[2] < count - 1e-13:
                        h, c, count = out
                        if count < best_count - 1e-14:
                            best_h = h.copy()
                            best_c = c.copy()
                            best_count = count
                        improved = True
                        if best_count - count < 1e-7 and rho > rho_min * 10.0:
                            rho = max(rho_min, rho * 0.8)
                    else:
                        out2 = line_search_direction(h, -d, count)
                        if out2 is not None and out2[2] < count - 1e-13:
                            h, c, count = out2
                            if count < best_count - 1e-14:
                                best_h = h.copy()
                                best_c = c.copy()
                                best_count = count
                            improved = True

        if improved:
            stall = 0
            continue

        stall += 1
        if stall == 1:
            rho = max(rho_min, rho * 0.85)
        elif stall >= 3:
            noise = max(1e-4, 0.25 * rho)
            if rng.random() < 0.6 or count > best_count + 1e-6:
                base = best_h.copy()
            else:
                base = h.copy()
            h_try = project_exact(base + noise * rng.randn(n), n)
            c_try = eval_corr(h_try)
            cnt_try = float(c_try.max())

            allow = max(1e-3, 0.05 + 0.001 * best_count)
            if cnt_try <= best_count + allow:
                h, c, count = h_try, c_try, cnt_try
            else:
                h, c, count = best_h.copy(), best_c.copy(), best_count

            rho = max(rho_min, rho * 0.7)
            stall = 0

    h = project_exact(best_h, n)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    count = float(c.max())
    if count < best_count - 1e-14:
        best_h = h.copy()
        best_c = c.copy()
        best_count = count
    return best_h, best_c, best_count


def resample_h(h, old_n, new_n):
    old_n = int(old_n)
    new_n = int(new_n)
    if new_n == old_n:
        return project_exact(h, new_n)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, old_n)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, new_n)
    out = np.interp(xn, xo, np.asarray(h, dtype=float))
    return project_exact(out, new_n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(120.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + max(60.0, budget - 12.0)

    try:
        h = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        n = int(h.size)
    except Exception:
        n = 400
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)

    if n <= 0:
        n = 400
        h = np.full(n, 0.5)

    h = project_exact(h, n)
    c = eval_corr(h)
    count = float(c.max())
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = c.copy()
    best_n = n
    best_count = count
    best_C = count * (2.0 / n)

    # Main linearized minimax polish on the native resolution.
    p_lp = t0 + 0.67 * budget
    if time.time() < p_lp - 20.0:
        h1, c1, cnt1 = lp_polish(
            best_h, best_c, best_n,
            deadline=min(p_lp, deadline - 5.0),
            max_qps=2000,
            seed=int(seed),
            rho_start=0.12,
            qp_time_limit=18.0,
        )
        if c5_of_corr(c1, best_n) < best_C - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c, best_n, best_count, best_C = h1, c1, best_n, cnt1, c5_of_corr(c1, best_n)

    # A second LP pass with smaller trust regions around the current best.
    p_lp2 = t0 + 0.78 * budget
    if time.time() < p_lp2 - 20.0:
        h2, c2, cnt2 = lp_polish(
            best_h, best_c, best_n,
            deadline=min(p_lp2, deadline - 5.0),
            max_qps=1500,
            seed=int(seed) + 101,
            rho_start=0.04,
            qp_time_limit=12.0,
        )
        if c5_of_corr(c2, best_n) < best_C - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c, best_n, best_count, best_C = h2, c2, best_n, cnt2, c5_of_corr(c2, best_n)

    # Scalar-programming polish with SLSQP on smooth maxima.
    p_slsqp = t0 + 0.86 * budget
    if time.time() < p_slsqp - 20.0:
        h3, c3, cnt3 = slsqp_polish(best_h, best_c, best_n, min(p_slsqp, deadline - 5.0))
        if c5_of_corr(c3, best_n) < best_C - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c, best_n, best_count, best_C = h3, c3, best_n, cnt3, c5_of_corr(c3, best_n)

    # Projected-gradient fallback / additional local refinement.
    p_grad = t0 + 0.90 * budget
    if time.time() < p_grad - 20.0:
        h4, c4, cnt4 = gradient_polish(best_h, best_c, best_n, min(p_grad, deadline - 5.0))
        if c5_of_corr(c4, best_n) < best_C - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c, best_n, best_count, best_C = h4, c4, best_n, cnt4, c5_of_corr(c4, best_n)

    # Optional higher-resolution refinement, only if still needed and time remains.
    if best_C > 0.3808 and time.time() < deadline - 220.0:
        nh = 800 if (deadline - time.time()) > 330.0 else 600
        hq = resample_h(best_h, best_n, nh)
        cq = eval_corr(hq)
        cntq = float(cq.max())
        hq, cq, cntq = lp_polish(
            hq, cq, nh,
            deadline=min(deadline - 160.0, time.time() + 160.0),
            max_qps=40,
            seed=int(seed) + 202,
            rho_start=0.05,
            qp_time_limit=6.0,
        )
        valq = c5_of_corr(cq, nh)
        if valq < best_C - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c, best_n, best_count, best_C = hq, cq, nh, cntq, valq

    # Final tiny-trust LP cleanup everywhere.
    if time.time() < deadline - 4.0:
        hf, cf, cntf = lp_polish(
            best_h, best_c, best_n,
            deadline=deadline - 1.0,
            max_qps=800,
            seed=int(seed) + 303,
            rho_start=0.015,
            qp_time_limit=6.0,
        )
        if c5_of_corr(cf, best_n) < best_C - 1e-15:
            best_h, best_c, best_n, best_count, best_C = hf, cf, best_n, cntf, c5_of_corr(cf, best_n)

    best_h = project_exact(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = eval_corr(best_h)
    best_C = c5_of_corr(best_c, best_n)

    return best_h, float(best_C), int(best_n)
```
